# Brand Census — every "nofx" occurrence + old logo (2026-08-20)

**Purpose:** complete inventory before the nofx → VL rebrand. Read-only — zero
code/config/asset changes. Owner reviews, rules per layer, then a SEPARATE
dispatch executes the renames.

**Scope note:** in-scope = 3,258 grep hits across tracked code/config/docs,
excluding `node_modules/`, build outputs (`dist/`, binaries `nofx`,
`nofx-bin`, `nq_smoke`, `sandbox-seed`, `.sandbox/nofx-sandbox`), `data/`,
`screenshots/`, `.playwright-mcp/` (41 files of local browser captures) and
`patches/` (generated bundle artifacts). Local files (`.env`, `.env.save`,
`.sandbox/` logs, machine-level systemd units) are listed separately —
values in `.env*` are masked (keys only).

## 1 · Counts summary

| Metric | Count |
|---|---|
| Total text hits (in-scope) | **3,258** |
| … L1 display / documentation | 1,349 |
| … L2 design tokens / assets / identifiers | 592 |
| … L3 infrastructure (module, imports, services, paths, env) | 977 |
| … not rendered (comments, tests, unclassified) | 340 |
| **Partner-shared** (file exists in partner fork or in the 264-patch bundle) | **2,710 / 3,258** |
| Per language | markdown 1,325 · go 1,059 · tsx 473 · ts 103 · sh 101 · yml 64 · css 26 · systemd units 22 · json/conf/misc ~87 |

Top hit classes: documentation text 1,172 · Go import path `nofx/…` 551 ·
Tailwind design-token class names `nofx-*` 354 · test strings 290 · infra
references (services/scripts/CI) 204 · markdown 153 · Go code identifiers 124
· deprecated `nofxos` provider identifiers 75 · i18n KEYS 70 (rendered values
already say VL) · `NofxSelect`-style component names 44 · comments 27.

**Headline:** the product UI is *already* VL-branded — browser tab title is
“VL Trader - AI Trading System”, the app logo is the VL monogram
(`web/public/icons/vl.svg`), and i18n values contain **zero** rendered
“NOFX” strings. What remains under the old name is mostly invisible
plumbing: design-token class names, i18n keys, the Go module path, service
names, log filenames, and docs.

## 5 · Coupling & risk map

| Coupling | Consumers | What breaks if renamed | Safe-rename procedure | Risk |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Go module path `nofx` (`go.mod`) + 551 `"nofx/…"` imports | every Go file, `logger` component derivation | compile-wide | mechanical import sweep in one commit; `logger` component names unchanged (derivation strips first path segment) | high |
| Binary names `nofx` / `nofx-bin` (`Makefile`, systemd ExecStart) | boot, deploy docs | unit won't start until ExecStart updated | rename binary + ExecStart + docs in lockstep | high |
| systemd units `nofx.service`, `nofx-web.service`, `nofx-backup.*`, `nofx-clock-guard.*` + journald `nofx.conf` | boot autostart, timers, journal tags | unit not found; timers stop | install renamed units, `daemon-reload`, enable; journal history keeps old unit names | high |
| Log filename `nofx_%s.log` (`logger/logger.go:90`) | rotated logs, log shipping (`log_events` unaffected) | new logs under new name; old logs keep old name | change format string; keep reader agnostic (it globs by pattern?) — verify in rename dispatch | medium |
| Env prefix `NOFX_*` (`.env` 3 keys; `NOFX_EXPECTED_REVISION` boot_integrity:87; `NOFX_CLOCK_STATE` clock_health:139) | boot integrity, clock guard, ports | boot asserts unset → inert; clock state path falls back to default | rename keys + `.env` + `.env.example` + readers in one deploy | medium |
| **C# wire** `Source: "nofx-go"` (Hello frame, `provider/ninjatrader/tcp_framing.go:102`, `tcp_server.go:1416`) | TCP Hello handshake — **C# does NOT validate the source string (verified: no `nofx-go` in the AddOn)** | nothing at runtime; protocol spec out of sync | rename + update `ninjascript/vltrader_tcp_PROTOCOL.md` in lockstep | low |
| C# `%USERPROFILE%\NofxTrader\account.txt` (`VLTraderTCPClient.cs:372,383`) | legacy CSV bridge account read (NT8 Windows box) | account resolution fails if folder renamed without AddOn change | requires AddOn edit → F5 → full NT8 restart + folder move on Windows | high |
| Tailwind tokens `nofx-*` (354 class hits; `tailwind.config`, `index.css`) | all styling | visual only | token rename sweep; screenshot-verify | low |
| `NofxSelect` component + `NofxOSBadge` | Strategy Studio | compile if renamed badly | rename component; badge text is display | low |
| i18n keys `whyChooseNofx` etc. (70) | translations | key mismatch = missing copy | key rename + all-locale update (values already VL) | low |
| log_events / journald WARN pipeline matchers | grep on “nofx”? — **no matcher greps the string “nofx”** (verified: pipeline matches levels/components, not brand) | nothing | n/a | none |
| Boot-integrity strings | `BOOT INTEGRITY OK/FAIL` + RELEASE rev — brand-free; only env key carries the name | nothing | env key row above | — |
| Docker: containers `nofx-trading`/`nofx-frontend`, GHCR images `nofx-*`, `nginx.conf` `proxy_pass http://nofx:8080` | CI registry, compose, nginx | registry/container mismatch, proxy 502 | rename image names + compose + nginx + GHCR repos together | high |
| Backup/restore paths `~/nofx-backups` + `deploy/RESTORE.md` | backups + runbook | restore lookups fail | keep path or migrate + update runbook | medium |
| Docs links to upstream `github.com/NoFxAiOS/nofx` (README badges, CHANGELOG, docs/community) | documentation | links 404 if upstream renamed (historical — upstream untouched) | docs pass rewrite | none |
| `nofxos` package/client identifiers (75) | deprecated provider (402-era, not live data) | unused path | leave as-is (deprecated) or sweep | none |

## 6 · Partner-fork impact (renaming = sync conflicts)

**2,710 of the 3,258 hits sit in files shared with the partner fork** (either
already in their tree or delivered by the 2026-08-20 patch bundle). Renaming
any of these creates a permanent divergence that every future format-patch
train must merge. Heaviest shared files with hits:

`kernel/engine.go` (46) · web pages TraderDashboard (95) / StrategyStudio (75)
· `CoinSourceEditor.tsx` (53) · `HeaderBar.tsx` (29) · `translations.ts` (72) ·
`index.css` (26) · `Makefile` · `logger/logger.go` (log filename) ·
`provider/ninjatrader/tcp_framing.go` (`nofx-go`) ·
`ninjascript/VLTraderTCPClient.cs` (`NofxTrader`) · deploy units ·
`.github/workflows`.

**Recommendation:** make the rebrand a *shared, mirrored* campaign — apply
the identical rename patches to the partner fork via the standing
format-patch flow (the fork's own branding files like `faqData.ts` stay
theirs), or explicitly accept the fork staying on old strings for L3 items
(module path, units, images) which its build already works with. C# changes
must respect the NT8 compile/deploy dance on their machine.

## 7 · L3 decision table (owner rules — no action taken)

| Item | Current | Proposed VL | Cost | Risk | Partner impact | Recommendation |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| systemd unit names (`nofx.service`, `nofx-web`, `nofx-backup.*`, `nofx-clock-guard.*`) | nofx-* | `vl-*` | small (reinstall units + daemon-reload) | medium | units live on his box too (separate install) | rename-later (own dispatch) |
| journald drop-in `nofx.conf` | nofx.conf | `vl.conf` | tiny | low | none | rename-later |
| Go module path `nofx` | nofx | `vl` (or `vltrader`) | medium (551 imports mechanical) | high if done sloppily | their fork shares the module — must mirror | rename-later, mirrored |
| binary name `nofx` / `nofx-bin` | nofx(-bin) | `vl` | small + ExecStart | medium | none (their box builds its own) | rename-later |
| DB/log paths `data/data.db`, `nofx_*.log`, `clock-guard-state.json`, `~/nofx-backups` | nofx_*.log etc. | `vl_*.log` etc. | small | low-medium (history retention) | none | rename-later (log pattern + backup dir) |
| env prefix `NOFX_*` | NOFX_* | `VL_*` | small (3+2 keys) | medium | none | rename-later with .env migration |
| Docker/GHCR images + nginx `proxy_pass http://nofx:8080` | nofx-* | vl-* | medium (registry re-tag) | medium | none | rename-later (deploy change) |
| GitHub repo name `johnwick2921-cyber/nofx` | nofx | vl… | small (redirects exist) | low | partner repo already `vlautoagenttraderv1` | owner choice |
| NT8 folder `NofxTrader` + AddOn file names | NofxTrader | VLTradertrader… | medium + NT8 redeploy | high | shared C# — must mirror | never-touch unless NT8 campaign |
| `NOFXi` assistant persona (telegram/chat brand) | NOFXi | owner ruling (keep? VLi?) | — | — | none | keep-forever unless owner renames the assistant product |
| `nofxos` deprecated provider identifiers | nofxos | — | zero | zero | none | keep-forever (dead code) |

## 8 · Residual set after an L1 + L2-only rename

If the first dispatch renames only display strings, design tokens, component
names and assets, the exact remainder would be:

- **L3 code/infra: 977 hits** — 551 import paths + module name, 204
  services/scripts/CI refs, 124 Go identifiers, 75 `nofxos`, 22 systemd-unit
  lines, 16 env-key reads, 8 log-filename lines, 4 `NofxTrader` C# sites,
  44 config refs, 23 unclassified.
- **Not rendered: 340 hits** — 290 test strings, 27 comments, 23 others.
- **Docs: 1,325 markdown hits** (1,172 documentation + 153 stray) — README,
  CHANGELOGs, docs/, plans, legal — a separate docs pass.
- **Machine-level:** all §2 systemd/backup/log paths + the Windows `NofxTrader`
  folder (unchanged by an L1/L2 pass).
- **Local files:** `.env`/`.env.save` keys, `.sandbox` logs/binaries, rotated
  `data/nofx_*.log`.

Net: after L1+L2, roughly **1,317 in-repo code/infra hits + 1,325 doc hits**
would still read “nofx”, plus all machine-level items — the visible product
would be fully VL while the plumbing waits for the owner's L3 rulings.
